## `AFG-4641-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01`

- **Role:** Seam-Slippage Metrologist
- **Scenario:** Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Textile Batch Spectrophotometer Shade Sorting & Weaving Defect

- **Standards:** `ISO 105`, `ISO 105`, `ASTM D2616-12 Standard Test Method for Evaluation of Visual Colo`, `ANSI/NCSL Z540.3-2006 Requirements for the Calibration of Measur`
